## Fees explanation

| Fee explanation |  |
| --- | --- |
| Name | Explanation |
| Order/adjustment ID | Transaction ID |
| Type | Order/adjustment |
| Order created time | Creation time of the order. |
| Order settled time | Time of settlement of the order to your account balance. |
| Currency | IDR |
| Total settlement amount | The final amount to be settled to your account balance as per calculated by the platform |
| Subtotal after seller discounts | The subtotal after seller discounts is the price after the seller's discounts are deducted |
| Subtotal before discounts | The subtotal before discounts is the total price before the seller's and TikTok Shop's discounts are deducted |
| Seller discounts | Seller discounts are promotions funded by the seller, such as coupons or product discounts |
| Distance item fee from Horizon+ Program | The distance fee for items paid by the customer. With your current settings for Horizon+ Program, this fee is included in product price for orders with shipping out of Java island. |
| Refund subtotal after seller discounts | The subtotal for refunded SKUs after seller discounts |
| Refund subtotal before seller discounts | The subtotal for refunded SKUs before seller discounts |
| Refund of seller discounts | The discount refunded to the seller |
| Platform commission fee |  |
| Pre-order service fee |  |
| Mall service fee | The Mall service fee ensures the convenient fulfillment of Mall orders. This fee is calculated based on the item price after seller discount x based category rate, or up to a maximum of Rp 50.000. |
| Payment Fee | The technical service fee charged for the platform's online payment, refund, settlement and other functions.(Payment fee =(customer payment - refund) * rate %)) |
| Shipping cost | The net shipping cost borne by the seller when there is a weight difference or the seller has provided a shipping cost discount to the customer. |
| Shipping costs passed on to the logistics provider | The shipping cost passed on to the logistics provider calculated based on the weight/dimensions measured by the provider |
| Replacement shipping fee (passed on to the customer) | Shipping fee cost passed on to the customer for replacement delivery |
| Exchange shipping fee (passed on to the customer) | Shipping fee cost passed on to the customer for exchange delivery |
| Shipping cost borne by the platform | The shipping cost incentive provided by the platform |
| Shipping cost paid by the customer | The shipping cost paid by the customer based on the product weight you uploaded |
| Refunded shipping cost paid by the customer | The shipping cost amount that was initially paid by the customer and has since been refunded to them |
| Return shipping costs (passed on to the customer) | The shipping cost passed on to the customer for return deliveries |
| Shipping cost subsidy | A positive amount represents a shipping cost subsidy you've received and a negative amount represents a shipping cost subsidy you need to return |
| Distance shipping fee from Horizon+ Program | The distance fee for shipping paid by the customer. With your current settings for Horizon+ Program, this fee is included in product price for orders with shipping out of Java island. |
| Logistics service fee | A fee charged throughout the order lifecycle for logistics-related services, including processing, coordination, handling of delivery-related matters, and basic shipping incentives |
| Affiliate Commission | (Product price (excl. tax) - Seller discount) * Commission rate. If refunds occur after order settlement, affiliate commission will not be refunded. |
| Affiliate partner commission | (Product price (excl. tax) - Seller discount) * Affiliate partner commission rate. If refunds occur after order settlement, affiliate partner commission will not be refunded. |
| Affiliate Shop Ads commission | (Product price (excl. tax) - Seller discount) * Ads commission rate. If refunds occur after order settlement, affiliate Shop Ads commission will not be refunded. |
| Affiliate Partner shop ads commission | The advertising commission cost a seller pays to |
